# Consolidated MSAJCE Knowledge Base QA Pairs

Total QA Pairs: 2546

## Source File: `msajce_about.md` (Category: About MSAJCE)

### Q1 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
When was the Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (MSAJCE) established?

**Answer**:
The Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (MSAJCE) was established on 5th July 2001.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
Who is the affiliating university of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE is affiliated to Anna University Chennai and the Government of Tamil Nadu.

---

### Q3 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
What is the campus area of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The MSAJCE campus sprawls over 70 acres of greenery located inside the SIPCOT IT Park in Siruseri.

---

### Q4 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
What are the infrastructure facilities available at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE has good infrastructure facilities such as spacious classrooms, drawing halls, laboratories, seminar halls, computer facilities with internet and wifi connectivity, a library, workshops, an auditorium, and a playground.

---

### Q5 (Exploratory) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
Why should I join MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE is located in a green, lush environment at the Siruseri IT Park, surrounded by over a hundred leading IT industries like TCS, CTS, Infosys, Intellect, FSS, and HCL. It offers state-of-the-art facilities with industry-collaborative technology centers.

---

### Q6 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
What kind of training does MSAJCE provide to its students?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE provides all kinds of training for the professional and overall transformation of the students, including internships, industrial visits, guest lectures, and seminars or webinars by industrial experts.

---

### Q7 (Yes_no) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
Does MSAJCE have a student exchange program?

**Answer**:
Yes, MSAJCE provides a student exchange program for cultural learning.

---

### Q8 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
What kind of language training does MSAJCE offer?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE features a communication language learning facility that offers training in English, German, and Japanese.

---

### Q9 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
What is the placement record of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE boasts a good placement record and provides robust support for higher education, helping students to become successful entrepreneurs.

---

### Q10 (Yes_no) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
Is MSAJCE connected by road, rail, and air?

**Answer**:
Yes, MSAJCE is well connected by road, rail, and air from various parts of the country.

---

### Q11 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — About & 1. Overview & 2. Why Join **MSAJCE**
What kind of projects do students at MSAJCE participate in?

**Answer**:
Students at MSAJCE are involved in various research and industrial projects, industrial hackathons, and project competitions that help them gain competency-based learning.

---

## Source File: `msajce_academicadvisorycommittee.md` (Category: General — MSAJCE)

### Q1 (Factoid) — Academic Advisory Committee & 1. Committee Members
Who is the Chairman of the Academic Advisory Committee at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Dr. K.S. Srinivasan, the Principal of MSAJCE in Chennai, serves as the Chairman of the Academic Advisory Committee.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — Academic Advisory Committee & 1. Committee Members
What is the role of Dr. K. Malathi in the Academic Advisory Committee?

**Answer**:
Dr. K. Malathi, Professor of Electronics and Communication Engineering at Anna University in Chennai, serves as an Advisor in the Academic Advisory Committee.

---

### Q3 (Factoid) — Academic Advisory Committee & 1. Committee Members
Who are the institutional heads from MSAJCE that serve as Members of the Academic Advisory Committee?

**Answer**:
The institutional heads from MSAJCE that serve as Members of the committee include the Head of Administration, the Head of Academics, the Head of Research, the Head of IQAC, the Head of Student Affairs, the Head of the Exam Cell, the Head of Placement, and the Head of Admission.

---

### Q4 (Exploratory) — Academic Advisory Committee & 1. Committee Members
What is the composition of the Academic Advisory Committee at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The committee includes the Principal of MSAJCE as the Chairman, several esteemed Advisors from various institutions and industries, and several institutional heads from MSAJCE as Members.

---

### Q5 (Factoid) — Academic Advisory Committee & 1. Committee Members
Who are the Advisors from industries in the Academic Advisory Committee?

**Answer**:
The Advisors from industries in the Academic Advisory Committee are Dr. N. Kulasekaran, Head of Research at Valeo India Pvt. Ltd, Mr. P. Parthasarathy, Senior Manager at Godrej and Boyce Manufacturing Co. Ltd., Mr. Ahamed Jameel A M T, an Alumni and Technical Consultant at HCL, and Mr. N. Feroz Khan, an Alumni and General Manager at Maersk.

---

### Q6 (Yes_no) — Academic Advisory Committee & 1. Committee Members
Are the Heads of all Departments at MSAJCE part of the Academic Advisory Committee?

**Answer**:
Yes, the Heads of all Departments at MSAJCE serve as Members of the Academic Advisory Committee.

---

### Q7 (Factoid) — Academic Advisory Committee & 1. Committee Members
What is the role of Dr. S. Thenmozhi in the Academic Advisory Committee?

**Answer**:
Dr. S. Thenmozhi, Professor of Civil Engineering at St. Joseph College of Engineering in Chennai, serves as an Advisor in the Academic Advisory Committee.

---

### Q8 (Factoid) — Academic Advisory Committee & 1. Committee Members
Who are the Advisors from academia in the Academic Advisory Committee?

**Answer**:
The Advisors from academia in the Academic Advisory Committee are Dr. K. Malathi, Professor of Electronics and Communication Engineering at Anna University, Dr. N. Parthiban, Professor of Computer Science and Engineering at SRM University, and Dr. S. Thenmozhi, Professor of Civil Engineering at St. Joseph College of Engineering.

---

## Source File: `msajce_admission.md` (Category: Admission & Fees)

### Q1 (Factoid) — 8. Postgraduate M.E and M.Tech Admission Eligibility & 9. Ph.D Mechanical Engineering Admission Eligibility & 10. Skill Development Course Admission
What is the qualification for admission to M.E in Computer Science and Engineering?

**Answer**:
A B.E or B.Tech in EEE, ECE, CSE, IT, I and C, E and I, IT, Electronics, or Instrumentation, or an M.C.A or an M.Sc 5 years integrated in IT, CSE, or Software Engineering in a 10 plus 2 plus 3 plus 3 years pattern.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — 8. Postgraduate M.E and M.Tech Admission Eligibility & 9. Ph.D Mechanical Engineering Admission Eligibility & 10. Skill Development Course Admission
How are students admitted for M.E Courses?

**Answer**:
Students are admitted by MSAJCE as per Government rules and regulations on the basis of the marks obtained by the candidates in their qualifying examinations.

---

### Q3 (Factoid) — 8. Postgraduate M.E and M.Tech Admission Eligibility & 9. Ph.D Mechanical Engineering Admission Eligibility & 10. Skill Development Course Admission
What is the alternative qualification for admission to Ph.D Mechanical Engineering?

**Answer**:
A Bachelor's Degree in Engineering or Technology of the University or any other qualification recognized as equivalent thereto in the fields of study with a minimum of 15 years of R and D Experience in National Research Laboratories or Public sector undertakings and with a minimum of three publications in referred impact factor journals or two approved international patents in the last five years.

---

### Q4 (Factoid) — 8. Postgraduate M.E and M.Tech Admission Eligibility & 9. Ph.D Mechanical Engineering Admission Eligibility & 10. Skill Development Course Admission
What is the minimum percentage of marks required for SC, ST, or Differently-Abled candidates for Ph.D Mechanical Engineering admission?

**Answer**:
50 percent marks or a CGPA of 5.0 on a 10 point scale.

---

1600 hours.

### Q10 (Factoid) — 5. Eligibility for Students from Other States & 6. Undergraduate B.E and B.Tech Admission Eligibility & 7. Direct Second Year Lateral Entry Eligibility
What is the eligibility criteria for students from Andhra Pradesh, Telangana, Kerala, and Northern States?

**Answer**:
The selection of candidates will be based on the qualifying marks obtained in Mathematics, Physics, and Chemistry at the Plus Two or Intermediate level.

---

### Q11 (Factoid) — 5. Eligibility for Students from Other States & 6. Undergraduate B.E and B.Tech Admission Eligibility & 7. Direct Second Year Lateral Entry Eligibility
How are the qualifying marks calculated for students from Andhra Pradesh, Telangana, Kerala, and Northern States?

**Answer**:
The qualifying marks are calculated as Mathematics divided by two, plus the sum of Physics and Chemistry divided by four.

---

## Source File: `msajce_antiragging.md` (Category: General — MSAJCE)

### Q1 (Factoid) — Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (**MSAJCE**), Siruseri, Chennai & 1. Punishments and Disciplinary Actions
What is the Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (MSAJCE)?

**Answer**:
The Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (MSAJCE) strictly prohibits any indiscipline activities by students that cause or are likely to cause annoyance, hardship, physical or psychological harm, or raise fear or apprehension in any fresher or other student.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (**MSAJCE**), Siruseri, Chennai & 1. Punishments and Disciplinary Actions
What are the consequences of ragging at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Students found guilty of ragging at MSAJCE face severe disciplinary actions and punishments, including withholding or withdrawing scholarships, debarment from appearing in tests, examinations, or other evaluation processes, and suspension or expulsion from MSAJCE hostel.

---

### Q3 (Yes_no) — Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (**MSAJCE**), Siruseri, Chennai & 1. Punishments and Disciplinary Actions
Is exploiting the services of any student to complete academic tasks a violation of the policy?

**Answer**:
Yes, exploiting the services of any student to complete academic tasks assigned to an individual or group is considered a severe violation of the policy.

---

### Q4 (Factoid) — Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (**MSAJCE**), Siruseri, Chennai & 1. Punishments and Disciplinary Actions
What types of physical abuse are prohibited under the policy?

**Answer**:
The policy maintains a zero-tolerance approach toward any physical abuse, encompassing all its variants such as sexual abuse, homosexual assaults, stripping, forcing obscene and lewd acts or gestures, causing bodily harm, or posing any danger to health or person.

---

### Q5 (Factoid) — Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (**MSAJCE**), Siruseri, Chennai & 1. Punishments and Disciplinary Actions
What are the punishments for severe ragging violations?

**Answer**:
More severe consequences for ragging violations include immediate suspension or expulsion from MSAJCE hostel, as well as the outright cancellation of their admission to MSAJCE. Depending on the severity of the offense, the administration may rusticate the student from MSAJCE for a period ranging from one to four semesters. In the most extreme cases, the punishment involves permanent expulsion from MSAJCE, along with consequent debarring from admission to any other educational institution for a specified period.

---

### Q6 (Yes_no) — Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (**MSAJCE**), Siruseri, Chennai & 1. Punishments and Disciplinary Actions
Is it forbidden to ask any student to perform acts they would not ordinarily do?

**Answer**:
Yes, it is strictly forbidden to ask any student to perform acts they would not ordinarily do, especially those that generate a sense of shame, torment, or embarrassment affecting their physical or psychological well-being.

---

### Q7 (Procedural) — Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (**MSAJCE**), Siruseri, Chennai & 1. Punishments and Disciplinary Actions
What is the procedure for dealing with students who engage in ragging?

**Answer**:
The administration reserves the right to withhold or withdraw scholarships, fellowships, and any other associated benefits from the offenders. Guilty individuals may be debarred from appearing in any tests, examinations, or other evaluation processes, and their academic results may be formally withheld.

---

### Q8 (Factoid) — Anti-Ragging Policy at Mohamed Sathak A.J. College of Engineering (**MSAJCE**), Siruseri, Chennai & 1. Punishments and Disciplinary Actions
What are the consequences of debarment from MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Debarment from MSAJCE may result in the student being unable to represent MSAJCE in any regional, national, or international meets, tournaments, or youth festivals.

---

### Q62 (Factoid) — Sectors with High Employment Potential & Key Skills Required for Cyber security Roles & Cybersecurity Certifications & Learning Paths for Cyber security Careers
What skills are required for network security roles?

**Answer**:
Network security requires an understanding of TCP/IP, network protocols, firewalls, and proficiency in tools like Wireshark, nmap, and Snort.

---

### Q63 (Factoid) — Sectors with High Employment Potential & Key Skills Required for Cyber security Roles & Cybersecurity Certifications & Learning Paths for Cyber security Careers
What skills are required for ethical hacking and penetration testing?

**Answer**:
Ethical hacking and penetration testing involve skills to simulate cyberattacks and uncover vulnerabilities using Kali Linux, Metasploit, Burp Suite, Nessus, and Aircrack-ng.

---

### Q64 (Factoid) — Sectors with High Employment Potential & Key Skills Required for Cyber security Roles & Cybersecurity Certifications & Learning Paths for Cyber security Careers
What skills are required for cloud security roles?

**Answer**:
Cloud security demands knowledge of securing environments like AWS, Azure, and Google Cloud, along with IAM and encryption.

---

### Q65 (Factoid) — Sectors with High Employment Potential & Key Skills Required for Cyber security Roles & Cybersecurity Certifications & Learning Paths for Cyber security Careers
What certifications can enhance credibility and job prospects in the field of cybersecurity?

**Answer**:
Notable certifications include the Certified Ethical Hacker by EC-Council, the Certified Information Systems Security Professional by ISC2, the Certified Information Security Manager and the Certified Information Systems Auditor by ISACA, and CompTIA Security+.

---

### Q66 (Exploratory) — Sectors with High Employment Potential & Key Skills Required for Cyber security Roles & Cybersecurity Certifications & Learning Paths for Cyber security Careers
What are the learning paths for cybersecurity careers?

**Answer**:
For beginners, the focus is on network security basics, firewall setup, and vulnerability scanning, often pursuing certifications like CompTIA Security+ and CEH Foundation through online platforms. At the intermediate level, the focus shifts to advanced network security, cloud security, penetration testing, and risk management, targeting certifications like CISSP, CISM, and CCSP through more in-depth training resources. Advanced level professionals focus on ethical hacking, security management, and incident response, aiming for certifications like CISA and CPTE through specialized training from organizations like EC-Council, GIAC, and the SANS Institute.

---

### Q67 (Factoid) — Sectors with High Employment Potential & Key Skills Required for Cyber security Roles & Cybersecurity Certifications & Learning Paths for Cyber security Careers

Incident response and forensics require skills to detect, respond, and recover from breaches, using digital forensics tools.

Data encryption and cryptography involve understanding encryption methods like AES, OpenSSL, and GPG to protect data.

### Q11 (Factoid) — Electronics and Communication Engineering with Specialization in Advanced Communication Technology & 1. Overview & 2. Programme & 3. Vision & 4. Mission
What are the subjects explored in the program?

Additionally, subjects like satellite communication, navigation systems, and quantum and molecular communication are also explored.

## Source File: `msajce_iqac.md` (Category: IQAC & Accreditation)

### Q1 (Exploratory) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What is the purpose of the Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC)?

**Answer**:
The prime task of the IQAC is to develop a system for conscious, consistent and catalytic improvement in the performance of institutions.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What is the objective of the IQAC?

**Answer**:
The primary objective is to develop a system for conscious, consistent and catalytic action to improve the academic and administrative performance of MSAJCE.

---

### Q3 (Exploratory) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What are the strategies of the IQAC?

**Answer**:
The IQAC shall evolve mechanisms and procedures for ensuring timely, efficient and progressive performance of academic, administrative and financial tasks.

---

### Q4 (Exploratory) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What are the functions of the IQAC?

**Answer**:
The functions include the development and application of quality benchmarks for the various academic and administrative activities of MSAJCE, and the dissemination of information on the various quality parameters of higher education.

---

### Q5 (Yes_no) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
Is the IQAC a hierarchical structure or a recordkeeping exercise?

**Answer**:
It will not be yet another hierarchical structure or recordkeeping exercise in MSAJCE, rather it will be a facilitative and participative voluntary system of MSAJCE.

---

### Q6 (Exploratory) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What is the role of the IQAC in the post-accreditation phase?

**Answer**:
The IQAC will make a significant and meaningful contribution in the post-accreditation phase of institutions. During the post-accreditation period, the IQAC will channelise the efforts and measures of an institution towards academic excellence.

---

### Q7 (Exploratory) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What is the potential of the IQAC?

**Answer**:
The IQAC has the potential to become a vehicle for ushering in quality by working out intervention strategies to remove deficiencies and enhance quality.

---

### Q8 (Exploratory) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What is the focus of the IQAC on academic and research programmes?

**Answer**:
The IQAC will focus on the relevance and quality of academic and research programmes, as well as equitable access to and affordability of academic programmes for various sections of society.

---

### Q9 (Exploratory) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What is the role of the IQAC in research sharing and networking?

**Answer**:
The IQAC is responsible for the optimization and integration of modern methods of teaching and learning, the credibility of evaluation procedures, and ensuring the adequacy, maintenance and functioning of the support structure and services. It also focuses on research sharing and networking with other institutions in India and abroad.

---

### Q10 (Exploratory) — Internal Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) & 1. Overview & 2. Objective of IQAC & 3. Strategies & 4. Functions
What is the purpose of the Annual Quality Assurance Report (AQAR)?

**Answer**:
The IQAC is responsible for the preparation of the Annual Quality Assurance Report (AQAR) to be submitted to NAAC based on the quality parameters.

---

The IQAC will facilitate a heightened level of clarity and focus in institutional functioning towards quality enhancement and facilitate internalization of the quality culture for NAAC for Quality and Excellence in Higher Education.

### Q1 (Factoid) — Kaushal Augmentation and Restructuring Mission of AICTE (KARMA) & 1. Overview and Objectives & 2. Skilling Models & 3. College Implementation and Approvals
What is the name of the AICTE initiative for skill development?

**Answer**:
The Kaushal Augmentation and Restructuring Mission of AICTE (KARMA)

---

### Q2 (Exploratory) — Kaushal Augmentation and Restructuring Mission of AICTE (KARMA) & 1. Overview and Objectives & 2. Skilling Models & 3. College Implementation and Approvals
What is the primary objective of the KARMA initiative?

**Answer**:
To overcome the dual challenge of the scarcity of skilled manpower in jobs and the low skill level of those who are presently employed.

---

### Q3 (Factoid) — Kaushal Augmentation and Restructuring Mission of AICTE (KARMA) & 1. Overview and Objectives & 2. Skilling Models & 3. College Implementation and Approvals
What is the focus of the training provided under the KARMA scheme?

**Answer**:
Domain-specific, demand-led skill training activities, preferably in core engineering sectors, leading directly to employment.

---

### Q4 (Factoid) — Kaushal Augmentation and Restructuring Mission of AICTE (KARMA) & 1. Overview and Objectives & 2. Skilling Models & 3. College Implementation and Approvals
What is the eligibility criteria for candidates to participate in the KARMA scheme?

**Answer**:
Candidates who have passed at least the tenth standard or completed ITI courses.

---

### Q5 (Exploratory) — Kaushal Augmentation and Restructuring Mission of AICTE (KARMA) & 1. Overview and Objectives & 2. Skilling Models & 3. College Implementation and Approvals
What are the three models proposed by AICTE for the skilling of in-school students, school dropouts, and students pursuing technical education?

**Answer**:
Model 1 targets school dropouts, Model 2 targets advanced-level skilling for students already studying in AICTE-approved technical institutions, and Model 3 integrates school education with higher education to skill students.

---

### Q6 (Factoid) — Kaushal Augmentation and Restructuring Mission of AICTE (KARMA) & 1. Overview and Objectives & 2. Skilling Models & 3. College Implementation and Approvals

Computer Hardware Network Maintenance and Refrigeration and Air Conditioning Technician.

### Q7 (Factoid) — Kaushal Augmentation and Restructuring Mission of AICTE (KARMA) & 1. Overview and Objectives & 2. Skilling Models & 3. College Implementation and Approvals

**Answer**:
AI and Machine Learning Developer, Refrigeration and Air Conditioning Technician, Additive Manufacturing Technician focusing on 3D printing, Certificate Course in Embedded System Design using ARM Cortex Microcontrollers, and Architectural Drafting and Basic 3D Design using Autodesk Revit.

## Source File: `msajce_msajcepolicy.md` (Category: General — MSAJCE)

### Q1 (Factoid) — Feedback Policy
What methods are used to collect student feedback?

**Answer**:
Oral feedback is collected by HODs at the first week of commencement of class, Feedback through class committee meeting conducted during 2nd and 10th week from the commencement of class, and Online feedback is collected in the middle of the semester.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — Feedback Policy
What is the scale used to rate the quality of teaching in online feedback?

**Answer**:
A scale of 5 (high) through 1 (low) is used to rate the quality of teaching in online feedback.

---

### Q3 (Factoid) — Feedback Policy
What types of feedback are collected from stakeholders?

**Answer**:
Feedbacks on Guest lecture/Seminar/Workshops/Industrial visit/Internship/etc are periodically collected from students and experts, and Feedback from parents, industry, alumni and visitors are collected and necessary actions are initiated.

---

### Q4 (Factoid) — Feedback Policy
What types of facilities are included in the feedback on facilities?

**Answer**:
Feedbacks on Infrastructure, Library, Housekeeping, Transport, Hostel, etc are collected periodically.

---

### Q5 (Exploratory) — Feedback Policy
What is the purpose of Academic Audit?

**Answer**:
The primary purpose of Academic Audit is to monitor and control the teaching-learning process and improve the quality of teaching.

---

### Q6 (Procedural) — Feedback Policy
What items are verified during the audit of course files?

**Answer**:
University syllabus and lesson plan of the corresponding subjects, Lecture notes / online materials/ other supporting materials of the corresponding subjects, Previous university question papers along with question bank, Assignment topics, Tutorial questions, The log book and track record of the lab sessions, CO attainment & PO attainment after the publication of results.

---

### Q7 (Exploratory) — Feedback Policy
What is the role of the Chairperson of the class committee?

**Answer**:
The Chairperson of the class committee convenes the meeting by inviting the Class advisor & subject handling faculty, student representative, prepares the minutes of every meeting, submits the same to the Academic review Committee within two days of the meeting, and arranges to circulate it among the students and teachers concerned.

---

### Q8 (Exploratory) — Feedback Policy
What are the functions of the class committee?

**Answer**:
The functions of the class committee include providing a forum for students to voice their academic grievances, solving problems experienced by students in the class room and in the laboratories, informing the student representatives, the academic schedule including the dates of Assessments and the syllabus coverage for each assessment.

---

### Q9 (Factoid) — HR Policies & 1. Definition & 2. Orientation & 3. Staff Dress Code & 4. Attendance Record
What is the effective date of the HR Policies for Mohamed Sathak AJ College of Engineering?

**Answer**:
These rules shall be called “Mohamed Sathak AJ College of Engineering”, Chennai, Policies, Conduct Rules, and Guidelines comes into force from the month of August 2019.

---

### Q10 (Factoid) — HR Policies & 1. Definition & 2. Orientation & 3. Staff Dress Code & 4. Attendance Record

**Answer**:
'Faculty Position' means a faculty carrying a definite scale of pay sanctioned without limit of time and included in the cadre of sanctioned position.

---

Every staff appointed in **MSAJCE** shall be given a brief introduction / orientation about **MSAJCE** by the Principal on the day of his / her joining.

---

The Manager (Admin) will also ensure that all the registration formalities, including submission of joining report etc., by obtaining the assistance of the Office team.

---

---

### Q16 (Exploratory) — HR Policies & 1. Definition & 2. Orientation & 3. Staff Dress Code & 4. Attendance Record

HOD will also take him/her on a tour to the campus, explaining him/her the various codes of conduct observed in availing the facilities in **MSAJCE**.

### Q31 (Factoid) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
Who is responsible for making appointments of faculty and staff members?

**Answer**:
The Chairman/Appointing Authority is responsible for making appointments of faculty and staff members as per the recommendation of the Selection Committee.

---

### Q32 (Factoid) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
How often is recruitment done at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Recruitment is normally done twice a year, during May and November.

---

### Q33 (Procedural) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
What is the process for screening applications?

**Answer**:
Screening of applications is done by the respective screening committee.

---

### Q34 (Factoid) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
What is the duration of the probation period for staff members?

**Answer**:
Every staff member appointed initially to a post, other than temporary appointments, shall be put on probation for a continuous period of one year.

---

### Q35 (Scenario) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
What happens if a staff member's performance is not satisfactory during the probation period?

**Answer**:
If the performance of the person appointed is not satisfactory during the period of probation, he will be issued a notice of warning at the end of the ninth month, and if his work continues to be unsatisfactory, his services will be terminated at the end of the one-year period.

---

### Q36 (Factoid) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
What is the basis for considering increment/promotions?

**Answer**:
Increment/promotions will be considered for those who completed one year of successful probationary period based on Self Appraisal.

---

### Q37 (Procedural) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
What documents are required for verification during the interview/joining process?

**Answer**:
Photo copies of all certificates, including UG, PG, and Ph.D. certificates, Degree or Diploma Certificate, Proof of DoB/X std mark sheet, Passport Sized photo, Experience certificates, Aadhar card, PAN card, and Community Certificate (if applicable), will be collected by HR/Admin Office and verified with original copy at the time of interview/joining and returned the same.

---

### Q38 (Exploratory) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
What is the role of the Selection Committee?

**Answer**:
The Selection Committee shall be constituted by the principal as per the guidelines approved by the Governing Council, and shall conduct interview and select the suitable candidates based on the performance and requirement.

---

### Q39 (Procedural) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
What is the process for issuing an offer letter to selected candidates?

**Answer**:
The offer letter will be issued for the selected candidates with specified date of joining.

---

### Q40 (Factoid) — 5. Recruitment / Interview Process of Staff & 6. Probation Period & 7. Increment / Promotions
What is the basis for fixing pay for selected candidates?

**Answer**:
Pay for the selected candidates shall be fixed as per the AICTE norms for the respective post, and as approved by the Governing Council based upon the qualification and experience of the candidate and other achievements.

---

### Q41 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
What are the reasons for termination of service at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The Chairman / Appointing Authority has the power to terminate the services of a member of MSAJCE for any of the following reasons: Serious misconduct and willful negligence of duty, Gross insubordination, Physical or mental unfitness, Participation in any criminal offence involving moral turpitude, or if the efficiency of an employee has been impaired due to any infirmity.

---

### Q42 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
What is the notice period for termination of service for a staff member on temporary appointments or during probationary period?

**Answer**:
One month notice or one month salary in lieu thereof.

---

### Q43 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
What is the notice period for termination of service for a staff member on permanent service?

**Answer**:
One month notice or one month salary in lieu thereof.

---

### Q44 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
What is the Casual Leave (CL) policy at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
All employees are eligible for 12 days of Casual leave per year at the rate of 1 day per working month during the year from 1st July to 30th June.

---

### Q45 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
How many Casual Leave (CL) days can be granted at a time?

**Answer**:
Not more than 3 CL shall be granted.

---

### Q46 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
Can Casual Leave (CL) be carried over to the next academic year?

**Answer**:
No, carryover of lapsed CL to next academic year is not permissible.

---

### Q47 (Procedural) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
What is the procedure for short absence not exceeding one hour on any working day?

**Answer**:
Permission for short absence not exceeding one hour on any working day either at the beginning of working hour, end of working hour or in the middle may be granted at the discretion of the Principal / Designated Authority for two occurrences per month.

---

### Q48 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
What happens if the number of permissions for short absence exceeds more than two in a month?

**Answer**:
It shall be considered as half day (1/2 day) CL for each permission exceeding two.

---

### Q49 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
What happens to excess Casual Leave (CL) taken while leaving service?

**Answer**:
Excess CL taken will be considered LOP at the time of settlement.

---

### Q50 (Factoid) — 8. Termination of Service / Resignation & 9. Leave Rules & 10. Casual Leave (CL)
Can Casual Leave (CL) be combined with any other leaves?

**Answer**:
No, CL cannot be combined with any other leaves except SL and Compensation Off.

---

### Q51 (Factoid) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
What is the maximum number of vacation leave days for faculty members?

**Answer**:
The total number of VL days for members of faculty (teaching staff) is limited to 30 calendar days.

---

### Q52 (Yes_no) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
Can vacation leave be availed in the winter vacation period?

**Answer**:
Yes, a maximum of 10 days can be availed in the winter vacation period in the months of November or December.

---

### Q53 (Scenario) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
What happens if a staff member avails vacation leave and does not complete the full term of 6 months or one semester?

**Answer**:
The leave availed will be treated as Leave on Loss of Pay (LOP) and proportionate salary will be deducted from any payment due to him/her.

---

### Q54 (Yes_no) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
Can vacation leave be combined with any other leave?

**Answer**:
No, vacation leave cannot be combined with any other leave.

---

### Q55 (Factoid) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
How many restricted holidays can a staff member avail in a year?

**Answer**:
Staff can also avail two such declared restricted holidays in a year by submitting proper leave letter after making alternation of his / her classes through HOD to the Principal.

---

### Q56 (Factoid) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
What is the maximum number of special leave days that can be availed for marriage?

**Answer**:
Special leave to a maximum of seven consecutive days (including holidays) may be availed for marriage of employees on production of marriage invitation and permission from the principal.

---

### Q57 (Yes_no) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
Can special leave be combined with any other leave?

**Answer**:
Yes, special leave can be combined with CL only.

---

### Q58 (Factoid) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
What is the maximum number of days of maternity leave that can be availed?

**Answer**:
A woman employee of MSAJCE, on successful completion of the probation period and having at least three years of continuous and satisfactory service, is eligible for Maternity Leave (ML) for a maximum of 180 days.

---

### Q59 (Yes_no) — 11. Vacation Leave (VL) & 12. Restricted Holiday (RH) & 13. Special Leave (SL) & 14. Compensation Off (C- Off) & 15. Maternity Leave (ML)
Can maternity leave be availed without prior approval?

**Answer**:
No, prior approval of the Principal / Designated Authority is required for maternity leave.

---

### Q60 (Factoid) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
What percentage of patent renewal fees is paid by MSAJCE for the first seven years?

**Answer**:
100% of the patent renewal fees will be paid by MSAJCE for the first seven years in all cases when the patent is taken by the college name.

---

### Q61 (Factoid) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
What happens if the patent is taken jointly with a sponsoring agency?

**Answer**:
If the patent is taken jointly with a sponsoring agency, the patenting cost will be equally shared.

---

### Q62 (Factoid) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
What are the options for MSAJCE if the other agency does not show interest in the patent process?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE can either continue the patent by paying the fees for its full term or withdraw the application for the patent protection, at its discretion.

---

### Q63 (Factoid) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
What percentage of the total revenue from commercial exploitation of IP is credited to inventors?

**Answer**:
30% of the total revenue accruing from the commercial exploitation of IP owned by MSAJCE shall be credited to inventors.

---

### Q64 (Factoid) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
What percentage of the total revenue from commercial exploitation of IP is credited to MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
70% of the revenue shall be credited to MSAJCE.

---

### Q65 (Factoid) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
Who bears the charges incurred for processing and acquisition of intellectual property rights?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE bears the charges incurred for processing and acquisition of intellectual property rights.

---

### Q66 (Factoid) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
What is the duration for which MSAJCE bears the maintenance charges for the IP?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE bears the maintenance charges for the first 7 years or till commercialization, whichever is earlier.

---

### Q67 (Yes_no) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
Can the ownership of IP be shared with the prior consent of the inventor?

**Answer**:
Yes, MSAJCE shall anytime share the ownership of the IP with the prior consent with the inventor.

---

### Q68 (Factoid) — 26. Patent fee & 27. Revenue sharing & 28. Conversion/Transfer of IP
What is the condition for the inventor to become a co-owner of the invention?

**Answer**:
In such condition, the inventor shall be the co-owner of the invention and MSAJCE shall be instrumental in the process of conversion.

---

### Q69 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the age limit for retirement of teaching staff?

**Answer**:
As per the guidelines of UGC/AICTE, teaching staff will retire at the age of 65.

---

### Q70 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the age limit for retirement of non-teaching staff?

**Answer**:
As per the government norms, non-teaching staff will retire at the age of 58 years.

---

### Q71 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the condition for extension of retirement for non-teaching staff?

**Answer**:
Two years of extension will be considered based on their physical fitness and service records.

---

### Q72 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for staff transfer within group of institutions?

**Answer**:
Staff members should accept to work in any of our group institutions based on the requirement.

---

### Q73 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What are the criteria for awarding merit certificates to students?

**Answer**:
Students who secure 1st three places (year wise) in University Exam and students having 100% attendance in each academic year will be awarded with Merit Certificates.

---

### Q74 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What are the criteria for awarding appreciation certificates to staff members?

**Answer**:
Staff Members producing 100% in the Theory Subjects during the Anna University Examinations will be awarded with appreciation certificates.

---

### Q75 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What are the activities that faculty/students will be awarded merit certificates/incentives/memento for?

**Answer**:
Presenting paper in International Conference and Journals, University Rank holders & Sports Achievements, Publishing books, Patents and Copy rights, Best outgoing student in UG and PG, Best alumni, Completion of Industrial consultancy projects and funded projects, Won prizes in various National and International competitions.

---

### Q76 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for patent and IPR ownership?

**Answer**:
The inventors shall be the owner for all the intellectual property inventions, which includes patent and the inventions invented or created by the inventors/creators who include faculty members, research, scholars, students and those who make use of the resources of the Mohamed Sathak AJ College of Engineering, Chennai.

---

### Q77 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the revenue sharing ratio between the inventor and the college for inventions created without using college resources?

**Answer**:
The revenue generated out of such inventions shall be shared in the ratio of 75:25 between the inventor and College respectively.

---

### Q78 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for patent and IPR ownership in case of institutional/industrial consultancy?

**Answer**:
If an IP has emerged as a result of an Institutional/Industrial consultancy, sponsored to Mohamed Sathak AJ College of Engineering, Chennai the concerned industries and Mohamed Sathak AJ College of Engineering Chennai shall own the IP.

---

### Q79 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for patent and IPR ownership in case of specific Memorandum of Understandings (MoU's)?

**Answer**:
This however will not apply to those IP that are covered under specific Memorandum of Understandings (MoU’s) where the action shall be carried out as per the provisions of the MoU’s.

---

### Q80 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for patent and IPR ownership in case of outside agency sponsored funds?

**Answer**:
If the IP is a result of funds sponsored by an outside agency, then the IP will be shared the Mohamed Sathak AJ College of Engineering, Chennai and the sponsoring agency on case-by-case basis, as per MoU / Agreement/Undertaking.

---

### Q81 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for staff resignation?

**Answer**:
No staff will be permitted to leave during the middle of the semester. For every staff exit feedback will be conducted before obtaining the resignation letter.

---

### Q82 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for experience certificate issuance?

**Answer**:
Experience certificate will not be issued for those who have not completed the successful probationary period.

---

### Q83 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for handing over document submission?

**Answer**:
Along with no due certificate, staff should submit the handing over/(list) document taken over.

---

### Q84 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for undertaking letter submission?

**Answer**:
Undertaking letter will be obtained from the staff for settlement.

---

### Q85 (Factoid) — 20. Relieving Policies & 21. Retirement Policies & 22. Transfer within group of Institutions & 23. Awards / Incentives for faculty, staff and students & 24. Higher studies & 25. Patent and IPR
What is the policy for encouraging faculty members for higher studies?

**Answer**:
The management shall encourage faculty members to upgrade their knowledge and in this context shall undertake to bear the cost of higher academic qualification or special training of faculty members after signing a necessary conditional bond to serve MSAJCE for a certain period after benefitting from such academic qualification/training.

---

### Q86 (Factoid) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is considered as Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP)?

**Answer**:
Absences without permission and without making alternative arrangement for class or other important duty will be treated as LOP. If any staff member is absent from duty without prior or later permission, exceeding 7 days, such period of absence will be considered as LOP.

---

### Q87 (Factoid) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What happens if a staff member is absent for more than 10 days?

**Answer**:
Such absence will also be considered as a Break-in-Service and in case exceeding 10 days will be considered for relieving from service.

---

### Q88 (Factoid) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the maximum number of days for On-Duty (OD) for university practical exam duty, theory papers evaluation, seminar/conference/workshop participation?

**Answer**:
The number of days for such OD is limited to 14 calendar days per semester.

---

### Q89 (Factoid) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What happens if a faculty member avails OD in excess of 14 calendar days per semester?

**Answer**:
The excess days shall be deducted from other eligible leave at credit of the staff and remaining days will be treated as LOP.

---

### Q90 (Factoid) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the additional OD that a faculty member is eligible for in a year?

**Answer**:
A faculty member is eligible for 14 calendar days of OD in a year for officially invited Anna University external examination invigilation works, AUR and examination squad duty.

---

### Q91 (Factoid) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the deadline for submitting an attendance certificate for regularization of OD?

**Answer**:
An attendance certificate obtained from the respective organization need to be submitted within two working days.

---

### Q92 (Yes_no) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
Can the Principal/Designated Authority cancel OD sanctioned earlier for any emergency work in MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Yes, the Principal/Designated Authority shall have the right to cancel the OD sanctioned earlier, for any emergency work in MSAJCE.

---

### Q93 (Procedural) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What should a faculty member do if there is any allowed expense during OD?

**Answer**:
He/she must get prior approval from the principal and detail of account should be settled on the next immediate day of reporting to duty.

---

### Q94 (Procedural) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is expected of a faculty member in terms of reporting and feedback during OD?

**Answer**:
The faculty should report to the concerned HOD daily and feedback should be given through mail.

---

### Q95 (Factoid) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What happens if a faculty member fails to submit an attendance report on the reporting day?

**Answer**:
OD's will be considered as LOP.

---

### Q96 (Procedural) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is expected of a faculty member who is deputed for specific purpose on 'Other Duty'?

**Answer**:
They should submit a detailed report to the principal about the purpose for which they are deputed, on the next day without fail.

---

### Q97 (Exploratory) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the expected conduct and discipline of every employee in MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Every employee shall, always, maintain absolute integrity and devotion to duty and do nothing which is unbecoming for an employee of an educational institution.

---

### Q98 (Exploratory) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the expected behavior of every employee in terms of courtesy and attention to all persons?

**Answer**:
Every employee shall extend utmost courtesy and attention to all persons with whom he/she is to deal in with the course of his/her duties.

---

### Q99 (Exploratory) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the expected behavior of every employee in terms of promoting the interest of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Every employee shall endeavor to promote the interest of MSAJCE and shall not act in any manner prejudicial thereto.

---

### Q100 (Yes_no) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
Can an employee join or continue to be a member of an association for the objectives or activities of which are prejudicial to the interests of the sovereignty and integrity of India or public order or morality?

**Answer**:
No, an employee shall not join, or continue to be a member of an association for the objectives or activities of which are prejudicial to the interests of the sovereignty and integrity of India or public order or morality.

---

### Q101 (Exploratory) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the expected behavior of every employee in terms of engaging in trade or business or undertaking other employment?

**Answer**:
No employee shall engage directly or indirectly in any trade or business or undertake any other employment.

---

### Q102 (Exploratory) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the expected behavior of every employee in terms of intoxicating drink or drug during course of duty?

**Answer**:
An employee of MSAJCE shall strictly abide by any law relating to intoxicating drink or drug during course of his/her duty.

---

### Q103 (Exploratory) — 16. Leave with Loss of Pay (LOP) & 17. On - Duty (OD) & 18. Conduct & Discipline & 19. Obligation to maintain secrecy
What is the obligation of every employee in terms of maintaining secrecy regarding MSAJCE's affairs?

**Answer**:
Every employee shall maintain the strictest secrecy regarding MSAJCE's affairs and the affairs of its constituents and shall not divulge, directly or indirectly, any information of confidential nature either to a member of the public or of MSAJCE's staff.

---

### Q104 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

Exchange of classes is not allowed, however in emergency cases can be made only with prior approval of the HODs of the concerned faculty members.

---

### Q105 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

The faculty –in- charge should keep the students in discipline. Library should not be used for discussion between students and faculty.

### Q106 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

Don't allow the students to use the Mobile phone inside the class room / Laboratories.

---

### Q107 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the responsibility of faculty members regarding the dress code of students?

**Answer**:
All faculty are advised to monitor the student’s dress code.

---

### Q108 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the expected behavior of faculty members during class hours?

Be in the class room in time and maintain strict discipline and silent during the lecture.

---

### Q109 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What should be done if a student misbehaves in the classroom?

Kindly bring to the notice of HOD / Principal immediately.

---

### Q110 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

Be cordial and don’t be partial in the class, interact with all the students and find their expectations and sort out the issues.

### Q111 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

### Q112 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

All non – teaching staff should be punctual to duty and should adhere to **MSAJCE** timings.

### Q113 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

### Q114 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

He / She is solely responsible for the all the equipment’s / machines and other materials available in the labs.

### Q115 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

If anything is missing / lost it should be brought to the notice of concern HoD`s / Principal immediately, otherwise it will be recovered from your salary.

### Q116 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

All the lab assistants are instructed to seal your lab with your sign and date.

### Q117 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

Don’t entertain any sweepers / attenders to clean the lab or machines in your absence.

### Q118 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)

Timely completion of Syllabus as per lesson plan, deviation if any should get approval from the HoD.

### Q119 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the responsibility of students regarding notebooks?

**Answer**:
For each subject, a note book must be maintained by the students.

### Q120 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the responsibility of faculty members regarding notebooks?

**Answer**:
During class hours, verify note books of at least 5 students daily.

---

### Q121 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the expected behavior of faculty members towards research?

**Answer**:
Publish minimum 3 research paper / year in the SCI the SCI / WoS /Scopus / UGC Journal.

---

### Q122 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the responsibility of faculty members regarding departmental activities?

**Answer**:
Coordinate with all the activities of Department and Institutional development.

---

### Q123 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the responsibility of faculty members regarding student participation in projects?

**Answer**:
Motivate all the interested students to participate in various industrial projects and competitions.

---

### Q124 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the responsibility of faculty members regarding their skills?

**Answer**:
Attend the FDPs to upgrade your skills.

---

### Q125 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the responsibility of faculty members regarding workshops and seminars?

**Answer**:
Plan and coordinate / associate to conduct a workshop / seminar / FDP / conference and social activities in the department.

---

### Q126 (Factoid) — Code of Conduct (Teaching Faculty)
What is the responsibility of faculty members regarding maintaining the status of the department?

**Answer**:
Maintain the status of the department and institute by actively involving in all the activity and maintain the self-discipline and student’s discipline.

---

### Q127 (Factoid) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
What is the policy on ragging in Mohamed Sathak AJ College of Engineering?

**Answer**:
Ragging in any form is totally prohibited in and outside the campus of Mohamed Sathak AJ College of Engineering.

---

### Q128 (Factoid) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
What is the objective of the Management, Faculty, and Staff members of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The objective is to make MSAJCE a 'Ragging free Institution' and ensure a conducive environment for freshers to adapt to the changes that MSAJCE life may demand on them and grow up along with their seniors.

---

### Q129 (Factoid) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
What are the regulations that MSAJCE follows to curb ragging?

**Answer**:
MSAJCE follows the UGC Regulations on curbing the menace of ragging in Higher Educational Institutions, 2009.

---

### Q130 (Factoid) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
What is the punishment for a student found guilty of ragging?

**Answer**:
A student found guilty of ragging can be expelled from MSAJCE, banned from the mess/hostel, have their scholarship withdrawn, be debarred from examinations, denied admission to any institution, and prosecuted for criminal action.

---

### Q131 (Scenario) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
What should a student do if they are being ragged?

**Answer**:
The student should immediately call the UGC Anti-Ragging Helpline (1800-1805522) or send an email to helpline@antiragging.in.

---

### Q132 (Yes_no) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
Is ragging in any form banned in MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Yes, ragging in any form is totally banned in MSAJCE.

---

### Q133 (Exploratory) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
What are the acts that constitute ragging?

**Answer**:
Ragging constitutes one or more of the following acts: teasing, ill-treating, or handling with rudeness a fresher or any other student; indulging in indiscipline activities that cause or are likely to cause annoyance, hardship, physical or psychological harm, or to raise fear or apprehension in any fresher or any other student.

---

### Q134 (Procedural) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
What is the procedure for submitting an affidavit at the time of admission?

**Answer**:
All students and parents should submit the affidavit at the time of admission as well as submit the online affidavit in the AICTE portal.

---

### Q135 (Factoid) — ANTI RAGGING POLICY
What is the contact information for the UGC Anti-Ragging Helpline?

**Answer**:
The contact information is 1800-1805522 (24/7 Toll Free) or helpline@antiragging.in.

---

### Q136 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What are the rules that administrative staff must follow at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
All administrative staff are bound to follow rules and regulations of MSAJCE and maintain strict discipline.

---

### Q137 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What happens if administrative staff are not punctual to duty?

**Answer**:
Administrative staff must be punctual to duty and strictly adhere to MSAJCE timings.

---

### Q138 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What are the timings for tea break and lunch break for administrative staff?

**Answer**:
Tea break and lunch break timings shall be strictly adhered to by the administrative staff.

---

### Q139 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What is the purpose of using computing and communication facilities for administrative staff?

**Answer**:
Computing and communication facilities should be used only for the purpose for which they are authorized to in accomplishing the assigned work.

---

### Q140 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What is expected of support staff in their interaction with students, teachers, parents, guardians, administrative personalities, general public?

**Answer**:
Support staff should demonstrate courtesy, respect, patience and willingness to help in all their interaction with students, teachers, parents, guardians, administrative personalities, general public in any context.

---

### Q141 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What is the expected behavior of administrative staff in the campus?

**Answer**:
Administrative staff are expected to maintain professional ethics in the campus and should maintain proper behavior with the authorities and students.

---

### Q142 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What are the restrictions on the use of mobile phones and listening to music, watching videos or surfing into any social media for administrative staff?

**Answer**:
Administrative staff are not allowed to use mobile phones and listen to music, watch videos or surf into any social media inside MSAJCE campus during the working hours.

---

### Q143 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What is the responsibility of administrative staff regarding cleanliness in the campus?

Maintain the cleanliness in their respective floor (class rooms, Labs, toilets etc..)

---

### Q144 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What should administrative staff do if they find any electrical, plumbing and carpentry work is pending in the floor?

**Answer**:
Action should initiate to sort out the issue immediately.

---

### Q145 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What is the policy regarding leave for faculty members?

**Answer**:
Free transportation shall be provided to all the faculty members to and from MSAJCE within the city limits (inclusive of the urban agglomeration). Avail leave only when it is a must and leave cannot be claimed as a matter of right.

---

### Q146 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
When should faculty members avoid taking leave?

**Answer**:
Avoid taking leave when the semester classes are going on.

---

### Q147 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions

**Answer**:
Break of service can be availed for Less than one year, beyond which if it exceeds one year, the faculty member should reappear for Interview and join the duty.

---

### Q148 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions

**Answer**:
Promotions and Increments are given to the eligible faculty / staff members after the successful completion of one year of service based on their overall performance appraisal.

---

### Q149 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
Are staff members allowed to pursue higher studies?

Staff members are permitted to pursue Higher studies, as part time program, while serving in MSAJCE.

---

### Q150 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What is the policy regarding Ph.D. for faculty members?

Faculty members are allowed to do Ph. D through Anna University /any other reputed Institute only.

---

### Q151 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
Who bears the expenses for submitting proposal and getting funding from funding agencies?

Management shall bear the expenses if any for submitting proposal and getting funding from the funding agencies.

---

### Q152 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What happens if faculty members are found dissuading from anything listed in these Rules?

**Answer**:
If found dissuading from anything listed in these Rules, the Management/Trust shall have the power and authority to decide and act upon any matter of concern that leads to chaos and arising difficulties.

---

### Q153 (Factoid) — Code Of Conduct (Administrative / Support Staff) & Other Terms and Conditions
What benefits are provided to faculty members?

**Answer**:
All the faculty covered with PF and Accident Insurance.

---

### Q154 (Factoid) — Scholarship Policy

**Answer**:
The minimum cutoff mark required to be eligible for the merit scholarship is 180 out of 200 marks.

---

### Q155 (Procedural) — Scholarship Policy

The students who are eligible have to fill an application form and the same has to be submitted to the Administration Office. The Scholarship committee will verify the authenticity of the certificates through a transparent approach and subsequently recommend the students to the trust for providing the eligible scholarship.

### Q156 (Yes_no) — Scholarship Policy

Yes, there is a special consideration of up to 10% in tuition fees for the girls students.

### Q158 (Factoid) — Scholarship Policy

The amount of scholarship provided by the Pragati scholarship scheme is Rs. 50,000 per year.

### Q159 (Yes_no) — Scholarship Policy

Yes, students who are from underprivileged background, despite their aggregate marks, are eligible for the scholarship.

### Q160 (Factoid) — Scholarship Policy

**Answer**:
There is no specific name mentioned in the context for the scholarship scheme for students from underprivileged background.

---

### Q161 (Factoid) — Scholarship Policy

The amount of scholarship provided by the Ministry of Minority Affairs is Rs. 20,000 per year for non-hostellers and Rs. 20,000 per year + Rs. 12,000 for hostellers.

### Q162 (Factoid) — Scholarship Policy
What is the name of the scholarship scheme for specially abled students?

**Answer**:
The name of the scholarship scheme for specially abled students is Saksham scholarship scheme.

---

### Q163 (Factoid) — Scholarship Policy
What is the amount of scholarship provided by the Saksham scholarship scheme?

**Answer**:
The amount of scholarship provided by the Saksham scholarship scheme is Rs. 50,000 per year.

### Q164 (Factoid) — Scholarship Policy

The name of the scholarship scheme for the wards of Beedi/Mine/Cine workers is Financial Assistance for Education to the wards of Beedi/Mine/Cine workers.

### Q165 (Factoid) — Scholarship Policy
What is the amount of scholarship provided by the Ministry of Labor & Employment?

**Answer**:
The amount of scholarship provided by the Ministry of Labor & Employment is Rs. 15,000 per year.

**Answer**:
Maintain the students database, Alumni database and placement records. To arrange internship, industry –sponsored major and minor projects for final year and pre-final year students in association with III cell coordinator. To maintain the directory of industries and research and development centres. To act as a information bureau by maintain libraries, data bases etc, relating to training and placement. Coordinating Alumni association activities, collect the feedback of the alumni from their working place. To maintain liaison with major corporate bodies like CII, FICCI, ASSOCHEM, etc., through industry institute interaction (IIIC) cell. Nurtures Industry Institute Interaction by organizing and coordinating frequent industrial visits, in plant training, projects of industrial relevance for the students with the sole aim of zeroing down the hiatus between the industry and the academia. Receives and forwards the feedback pertinent to curriculum improvement from the visiting companies to the faculty / HOD. To prepare and update the information brochure for MSAJCE. To guide in preparing resume by the students and train them to face interviews. To interact with industries and organize campus interview for pre-final and final year students with business houses of repute from all over India and prepare them for such interviews. To arrange seminars for the students on latest topics by the industry / business executives Organize Mock Interview, Group Discussions and HR interaction / conclave periodically. Coordinating Placement and ensuring all the students underwent Internship as per the AICTE / Anna University norms and creating awareness about Higher Education and EDP activities.

---

### Q333 (Exploratory) — Responsibility of Faculty Lab In charge & Responsibility of Faculty Handling laboratory classes
What are the responsibilities of a Faculty Lab In charge?

**Answer**:
Procurement, erection, installation and commissioning of lab equipments, procurement and storage of consumables, materials, tools and instruments, planning, scheduling, organizing and coordinating with staff handling lab classes, planning and organizing development program for supporting staff, managing the maintenance of equipments and tools in the lab, suggesting the removal of obsolete and condemned equipments, breakage list and explanations are to be sent to HOD at the end of every semester, stock register to be maintained by the lab-in-charge concerned for machinery / equipment and consumables, monitoring supporting staff in their work and maintaining discipline, recommending leave and permission for supporting staff, displaying rules and regulations, safety precautions for the students in the laboratory, intimating the electrical maintenance section in case of adding any electrical equipment, ensuring proper security of lab equipments, ensuring before leaving the campus locking of laboratory after college hour, switch off Electric power, air conditioners, fans, lights etc.

---

### Q334 (Exploratory) — Responsibility of Faculty Lab In charge & Responsibility of Faculty Handling laboratory classes
What are the responsibilities of a Faculty Handling laboratory classes?

**Answer**:
Responsible for conducting the lab classes for that semester and particular subject, one or two teaching staff will assist him/her, planning and designing the experiments to fulfil the curriculum, ensuring discipline and attendance of the students, explaining the theory and operation for the experiments, checking and approving the observation book and correcting Laboratory records, co-coordinating with Lab-in-charge for efficient and effective conducting of lab classes, making necessary arrangements for conducting University / model practical examinations, assessing the performance and finalizing the marks, while conducting special classes, ensuring proper transport and canteen facilities for students / staff, reporting to Lab-in-charge any damage of the equipment / defective then and there.

---

### Q335 (Procedural) — Responsibility of Faculty Lab In charge & Responsibility of Faculty Handling laboratory classes
What should be done in case of damage of equipment / defective during lab classes?

**Answer**:
Report to Lab-in-charge any damage of the equipment / defective then and there.

---

### Q336 (Exploratory) — Responsibility of Faculty Lab In charge & Responsibility of Faculty Handling laboratory classes
What is the responsibility of Assisting Faculty : Lab Assistant / Instructor / Programmer?

**Answer**:
Assisting the staff handling the lab classes in conducting experiments, guiding the students in the performance of practical task / exercise, ensuring the safety of the students, equipment and machinery while conducting lab class, assisting the students and faculty members in the fabrication of projects, making necessary arrangements for conducting university / model practical examinations, storage and accounting of raw materials, tools and instruments, arrangements for issuing of raw materials, tools and instruments for the experiment, taking the guidance and suggestion from the Lab-In-Charge for the periodic and preventive maintenance, numbering of equipment, painting, calibration etc., maintaining the lab by running the machinery periodically when the lab is free, any damage of accessories and equipments by students must be brought to the notice of lab-in-charge, not allowing the students in lab during their theory class, providing all assistance to the Lab-in-charge in maintaining and running the laboratory smoothly and ensuring safety and security of the lab.

---

### Q337 (Procedural) — Responsibility of Faculty Lab In charge & Responsibility of Faculty Handling laboratory classes
What should be done in case of missing / lost equipment?

**Answer**:
It should be brought to the notice of concern HoD`s / Principal immediately, otherwise it will be recovered from your salary.

He/She is responsible for opening and closing of their concern Labs.

### Q339 (Factoid) — Responsibility of Faculty Lab In charge & Responsibility of Faculty Handling laboratory classes

### Q340 (Procedural) — Responsibility of Faculty Lab In charge & Responsibility of Faculty Handling laboratory classes
