### Q1 (Factoid) — NAAC & 1. NATIONAL ASSESSMENT AND ACCREDITATION COUNCIL (NAAC)
What is the name of the council established in 1994?

**Answer**:
The National Assessment and Accreditation Council (NAAC) was established in 1994.

---

### Q2 (Exploratory) — NAAC & 1. NATIONAL ASSESSMENT AND ACCREDITATION COUNCIL (NAAC)
What is the primary mandate of the NAAC?

**Answer**:
The NAAC's vision statement reflects its core mandate, which is to integrate quality assurance into the functioning of Higher Education Institutions (HEIs), thereby ensuring a commitment to excellence in education.

---

### Q3 (Factoid) — NAAC & 1. NATIONAL ASSESSMENT AND ACCREDITATION COUNCIL (NAAC)
What are the two key bodies through which the NAAC operates?

**Answer**:
The NAAC operates through two key bodies: the General Council (GC) and the Executive Committee (EC), both of which comprise a diverse group of educational administrators, policymakers, and senior academicians from various segments of the Indian higher education system.

---

### Q4 (Factoid) — NAAC & 1. NATIONAL ASSESSMENT AND ACCREDITATION COUNCIL (NAAC)
Who serves as the President of the General Council?

**Answer**:
The Chairperson of the University Grants Commission (UGC) serves as the President of the GC.

---

### Q5 (Factoid) — NAAC & 1. NATIONAL ASSESSMENT AND ACCREDITATION COUNCIL (NAAC)
Who is the Chairperson of the Executive Committee?

**Answer**:
The Chairperson of the Executive Committee is an esteemed academician nominated by the President of the GC.

---

### Q6 (Exploratory) — NAAC & 1. NATIONAL ASSESSMENT AND ACCREDITATION COUNCIL (NAAC)
What is the role of the Director of the NAAC?

**Answer**:
The Director of the NAAC plays a pivotal role in the organization, serving as both the academic and administrative head. In addition to these responsibilities, the Director is also the member secretary of both the General Council and the Executive Committee, facilitating the smooth functioning of these bodies.

---

### Q7 (Yes_no) — NAAC & 1. NATIONAL ASSESSMENT AND ACCREDITATION COUNCIL (NAAC)
Does the NAAC seek guidance from advisory and consultative committees?

**Answer**:
Yes, the NAAC also seeks guidance from advisory and consultative committees that are constituted as needed. These committees provide valuable insights and expertise, enabling the NAAC to make informed decisions and develop effective policies.

---

### Q8 (Factoid) — NAAC & 1. NATIONAL ASSESSMENT AND ACCREDITATION COUNCIL (NAAC)
What is the location of the NAAC's headquarters?

**Answer**:
The NAAC's headquarters is located in Bengaluru.

### Q49 (Factoid) — 7. Code of Conduct
What is the minimum attendance percentage required for students to avoid detention from the end-semester examination?

**Answer**:
According to Anna University norms, students must maintain a minimum attendance of 75% in each semester.

---

### Q50 (Scenario) — 7. Code of Conduct
If a student is absent from class, what should they do to avoid detention?

**Answer**:
Students must obtain prior written permission for absence, and in cases of continuous absence due to medical reasons, they must submit a medical certificate.

---

### Q51 (Yes_no) — 7. Code of Conduct
Is the use of mobile phones and laptops allowed within the campus?

**Answer**:
Yes, the use of mobile phones and laptops is permitted within the campus, but students are not allowed to use these devices during class hours or examinations.

---

### Q52 (Procedural) — 7. Code of Conduct
What is the procedure for faculty members to evaluate assessment papers, assignments, and tutorial papers?

**Answer**:
Faculty members are expected to evaluate assessment papers, assignments, and tutorial papers on the same day and distribute the results to students in the next immediate class.

---

### Q53 (Procedural) — 7. Code of Conduct
How often should faculty members verify the notebooks of students?

**Answer**:
Faculty members are expected to verify the notebooks of at least five students daily during class hours to monitor their progress and provide feedback.

---

### Q54 (Exploratory) — 7. Code of Conduct
What is the responsibility of laboratory assistants in maintaining the laboratory equipment and materials?

**Answer**:
Laboratory assistants are solely responsible for all the equipment, machines, and other materials available in the labs. If any equipment or material is missing or lost, it is their duty to bring it to the notice of the concerned Head of Department (HoD) or Principal immediately.

---

### Q55 (Yes_no) — 7. Code of Conduct
What is the policy regarding the use of mobile phones by administrative staff members during working hours?

**Answer**:
Administrative staff members are strictly prohibited from using their mobile phones to listen to music, watch videos, or access social media while on college premises.

---

---

The National Institutional Ranking Framework (NIRF) is a methodology adopted by the Ministry of Education, Government of India, to rank institutions of higher education across the country.

MSAJCE participates in NIRF rankings annually and submits data across three categories, which are Engineering, Overall, and Innovation.

### Q1 (Factoid) — History and Leadership & 1. Mohamed Sathak Trust & 2. Our Founders & 3. Our Trustees & 4. Chairman Message
When was the Mohamed Sathak Trust established?

**Answer**:
The Mohamed Sathak Trust was established on the 26th of October 1973.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — History and Leadership & 1. Mohamed Sathak Trust & 2. Our Founders & 3. Our Trustees & 4. Chairman Message
What was the primary purpose of establishing the Mohamed Sathak Trust?

**Answer**:
The primary purpose of establishing the Mohamed Sathak Trust was to help deprived people from rural and financially challenged backgrounds to obtain quality education.

---

### Q3 (Factoid) — History and Leadership & 1. Mohamed Sathak Trust & 2. Our Founders & 3. Our Trustees & 4. Chairman Message
What was the first self-financing engineering college established by the Mohamed Sathak Trust?

**Answer**:
The first self-financing engineering college in the country was established by the Trust in 1984 at Kilakarai.

---

### Q4 (Factoid) — History and Leadership & 1. Mohamed Sathak Trust & 2. Our Founders & 3. Our Trustees & 4. Chairman Message
How many educational institutions does the Mohamed Sathak Trust presently have?

**Answer**:
The trust presently has eighteen educational institutions across Kilakarai, Ramanathapuram, and Chennai.

---

### Q5 (Factoid) — History and Leadership & 1. Mohamed Sathak Trust & 2. Our Founders & 3. Our Trustees & 4. Chairman Message
Who are the founders of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The founders of MSAJCE include Late Alhaj Dr. S. M. Dasthagir, Late Hajjani Syed Hameeda Beevi, Late Alhaj Dr. P.R.L. Shaik Aboobacker, Late Alhaj S.M. Ahamed Jalaluddin, Late Alhaj S. M. Kabeer, and Late Alhaj Dr. S. M. Hamid Abdul Quadir.

---

### Q6 (Factoid) — History and Leadership & 1. Mohamed Sathak Trust & 2. Our Founders & 3. Our Trustees & 4. Chairman Message
Who serves as the Chairman of the trustees guiding MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Alhaj Janab S. M. Yousuf serves as the Chairman.

---

### Q7 (Factoid) — History and Leadership & 1. Mohamed Sathak Trust & 2. Our Founders & 3. Our Trustees & 4. Chairman Message
What is the guiding philosophy of the Mohamed Sathak Trust?

**Answer**:

## Source File: `msajce_research.md` (Category: Research & Publications)

### Q1 (Exploratory) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
What are the objectives of the Research and Development Cell at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The Research and Development Cell aims to nurture research culture in MSAJCE by promoting research in newly emerging and challenging areas of Engineering, Technology, Science and Humanities. It also aims to create zeal among the students and faculty towards research and innovation, and to encourage them to publish their findings in conferences and journals.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
Who is the Head of Research at MSAJCE in Chennai?

**Answer**:
Dr. B. Janarthanan

---

### Q3 (Exploratory) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
What are the facilities and infrastructures provided by the Research and Development Cell to students and faculty?

**Answer**:
The cell provides necessary facilities and infrastructures required for carrying out research.

---

### Q4 (Exploratory) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
What is the purpose of the Research Advisory Committee at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The Research Advisory Committee is convened to assist the students to apply funding for conducting research under student project scheme to various funding agencies, and to work closely with the industry to understand their needs.

---

### Q5 (Factoid) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
Who are the Advisors of the Research Advisory Committee at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
Dr. K.S. Srinivasan, Dr. Parthiban, Dr. Somashekhar S Hiremath, and Dr. Sabitha Ramakrishnan

---

### Q6 (Exploratory) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
What is the role of the Research and Development Cell in promoting Institute-Industry Collaboration?

**Answer**:
The cell aims to work closely with the industry to understand their needs that eventually will result in new or improved products, processes, systems or services that can increase the company's productivity.

---

### Q7 (Factoid) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
What are the funding agencies that the Research and Development Cell assists the students to apply for?

**Answer**:
TNSCST, MSME, IEI, TNSCST, DST, AICTE, UGC, ISRO, DRDO, CSIR, and DBT

---

### Q8 (Exploratory) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
What are the activities organized by the Research and Development Cell to promote research?

**Answer**:
The cell will organize various workshops, seminars, and trainings related to promotion of research.

---

### Q9 (Exploratory) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee
What is the purpose of the Research and Development Cell in supporting the government?

**Answer**:
The cell aims to support the government in serving the society by taking up sponsored projects offered by agencies such as TNSCST, DST, AICTE, UGC, ISRO, DRDO, CSIR, and DBT.

---

### Q10 (Factoid) — Research & 1. Objectives & 2. Research Advisory Committee

**Answer**:
The mission of the department involves imparting fundamental knowledge and necessary skills in Science and Humanities through innovative teaching and learning methodologies.

---

### Q4 (Exploratory) — **MSAJCE** — Science And Humanities & 1. Overview & 2. Vision & 3. Mission & 4. Programme & 5. Faculty & 6. Laboratories
What are the career opportunities for graduates of the B.E. in Mechanical Engineering program?

**Answer**:
Career opportunities for graduates include roles such as Planners, Designers, Constructors and Operators, Stewards of the Environment, Innovators and Integrators of Technology, Managers of Risk, and Leaders in Public Policy.

---

### Q5 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — Science And Humanities & 1. Overview & 2. Vision & 3. Mission & 4. Programme & 5. Faculty & 6. Laboratories
What is the eligibility for the B.E. in Mechanical Engineering program?

**Answer**:
Eligibility for the program requires completion of the 10+2 system of education, and students must have secured a pass in Physics, Chemistry, and Mathematics in their qualifying examination.

---

### Q6 (Factoid) — **MSAJCE** — Science And Humanities & 1. Overview & 2. Vision & 3. Mission & 4. Programme & 5. Faculty & 6. Laboratories
What are the different types of students for the B.E. in Mechanical Engineering program?

**Answer**:
The duration is four years for regular students and three years for lateral entry students.

---

---

### Q10 (Exploratory) — 1. NSS
What is the primary goal of the NSS unit at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The primary objectives of the NSS unit are multifaceted, including working among the people, enhancing the knowledge of themselves and the community, and applying their studies to practical use in addressing real-world difficulties.

---

### Q11 (Factoid) — 1. NSS
What are some of the events organized by the NSS unit at MSAJCE?

The NSS unit has organized numerous events and activities, including blood donation camps, awareness programs on health and hygiene, consumer rights, environmental protection, and AIDS awareness programs.

---

### Q12 (Exploratory) — 1. NSS
What is the role of NSS volunteers in the community?

**Answer**:
NSS volunteers are expected to establish relationships with the society, identify needs, problems, and resources within the community, and relate their learning experiences to finding solutions to the problems identified.

---

### Q13 (Procedural) — 1. NSS
What is the code of conduct for NSS volunteers at MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
A code of conduct has been established for NSS volunteers, emphasizing the importance of working under the guidance of a group leader nominated by the program officer, maintaining the confidence and cooperation of the group and community leadership, and avoiding controversial issues.

---

### Q14 (Factoid) — 1. NSS

The NSS section organizes various events to commemorate national importance days, including National Youth Day, Independence Day, and Republic Day, as well as Road Safety Week.

---

---

---

### Q1 (Factoid) — Vision, Mission, and Quality Policy & 1. Vision & 2. Mission & 3. Quality Policy
What is the vision of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The vision of MSAJCE is to be an eminent institute for higher education and research through innovative teaching-learning and sustainable practices to meet industrial and societal needs.

---

### Q2 (Factoid) — Vision, Mission, and Quality Policy & 1. Vision & 2. Mission & 3. Quality Policy
What is the primary goal of the mission of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The primary goal of the mission of MSAJCE involves imparting quality technical education by transforming students into professionally competent technocrats.

---

### Q3 (Exploratory) — Vision, Mission, and Quality Policy & 1. Vision & 2. Mission & 3. Quality Policy
What does the mission of MSAJCE aim to achieve?

**Answer**:
The mission of MSAJCE aims to nurture the students in all-round sustainable development for their careers and self-employability, alongside instilling critical thinking, professional ethics, and civic responsibilities.

---

### Q4 (Factoid) — Vision, Mission, and Quality Policy & 1. Vision & 2. Mission & 3. Quality Policy
What is the focus of the quality policy of MSAJCE?

**Answer**:
The quality policy of MSAJCE is committed to creating quality professionals through innovative teaching, research, and industrial relations, while nurturing human values and ethics.

---

### Q5 (Factoid) — Vision, Mission, and Quality Policy & 1. Vision & 2. Mission & 3. Quality Policy
What does the quality policy of MSAJCE aim to meet?

**Answer**:
The quality policy of MSAJCE aims to meet industrial and societal needs on a continual improvement basis.

---

### Q6 (Exploratory) — Vision, Mission, and Quality Policy & 1. Vision & 2. Mission & 3. Quality Policy
What are the core values that the mission of MSAJCE seeks to instill?

**Answer**:
The mission of MSAJCE seeks to instill strong core values, including civic responsibilities, alongside critical thinking and professional ethics.

---
